- **codex**: Delegate work to OpenAI Codex CLI (ChatGPT quota, NOT your Claude plan). AUTO-routes repo exploration / read-only recon to gpt-5.4-mini medium · ON-TRIGGER ("use /codex &lt;action&gt;") routes implement / refactor / review / tests / bug hunts to gpt-5.4 high.
